# Aggregated political leader scripted effects [G-L], merged from per-country files.

# --- GAB_political_leaders.txt ---
set_leader_GAB = {

	if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 1 } }

		if = { limit = { check_variable = { conservatism_leader = 0 } }
			set_variable = { conservatism_leader = 1 }
			kill_country_leader = yes

			create_country_leader = {
				name = ZZZ_nm_gab_omar_bongo
				picture = "omar_bongo.dds"
				ideology = conservatism
				traits = {
					western_conservatism
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { conservatism_leader = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { date < 2016.1.2 } set_temp_variable = { b = 1 } } #skip if 2017
		}
	}
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 14 } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { Neutral_conservatism_leader = 0 } }
			set_variable = { Neutral_conservatism_leader = 1 }
			kill_country_leader = yes

			create_country_leader = {
				name = ZZZ_nm_gab_ali_bongo_ondimba
				picture = "Ali_Bongo_Ondimba.dds"
				ideology = Neutral_conservatism
				traits = {
					neutrality_Neutral_conservatism
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { Neutral_conservatism_leader = 1 } }
			set_temp_variable = { b = 1 }
		}
	}
}

# --- GAH_political_leaders.txt ---
set_leader_GAH = {

	if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { conservatism_leader = 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { conservatism_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = ZZZ_nm_gah_john_kufuor
				picture = "John Kufuor.dds"
				expire = "2005.01.07"
				ideology = conservatism
				traits = {
					western_conservatism
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { conservatism_leader = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { date < 2016.1.2 } set_temp_variable = { b = 1 } } #skip if 2017
		}
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { conservatism_leader = 1 } NOT = { check_variable = { b = 1 } } }
			add_to_variable = { conservatism_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = ZZZ_nm_gah_nana_akufo_addo
				picture = "Nana_Akufo-Addo.dds"
				ideology = conservatism
				traits = {
					western_conservatism
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { conservatism_leader = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { date < 2016.1.2 } set_temp_variable = { b = 1 } } #skip if 2017
		}
	}
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 2 } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { liberalism_leader = 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { liberalism_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = ZZZ_nm_gah_paa_kwesi_nduom
				picture = "Paa_Kwesi_Nduom.dds"
				ideology = liberalism
				traits = {
					western_liberalism

		if = { limit = { check_variable = { socialism_leader = 1 } NOT = { check_variable = { b = 1 } } }
			add_to_variable = { socialism_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = ZZZ_nm_gah_john_dramani_mahama
				picture = "John_Dramani_Mahama1.dds"
				ideology = socialism

			}

	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 4 } }

			create_country_leader = {

		if = { limit = { check_variable = { communist_state_leader = 1 } NOT = { check_variable = { b = 1 } } }

			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = ZZZ_nm_gah_george_aggudey
				picture = "George_Aggudey.dds"
				expire = "2005.01.07"
				ideology = communist_state
				traits = {
					emerging_communist_state
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { communist_state_leader = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { date < 2016.1.2 } set_temp_variable = { b = 1 } } #skip if 2017

		if = { limit = { check_variable = { communist_state_leader = 2 } NOT = { check_variable = { b = 1 } } }

# --- GAM_political_leaders.txt ---
set_leader_GAM = {
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 13 } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { Neutral_Autocracy_leader = 0 } }
			set_variable = { Neutral_Autocracy_leader = 1 }
			kill_country_leader = yes

			create_country_leader = {
				name = ZZZ_nm_gam_yahya_jammeh
				picture = "Yahya_Jammeh.dds"
				ideology = Neutral_Autocracy
				traits = {
					neutrality_Neutral_Autocracy
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { Neutral_Autocracy_leader = 1 } }
			set_temp_variable = { b = 1 }
		}
	}
}

# --- GEO_political_leaders.txt ---
set_leader_GEO = {

	if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 3 } }

		if = { limit = { check_variable = { socialism_leader = 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { socialism_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = GEO_shalva_natelashvili
				picture = "shalva_natelashvi.dds"
				ideology = socialism
				traits = {
					western_socialism
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { socialism_leader = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { date < 2016.1.2 } set_temp_variable = { b = 1 } } #skip if 2017
		}
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { socialism_leader = 1 } NOT = { check_variable = { b = 1 } } }
			add_to_variable = { socialism_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = GEO_irakli_garibashvili
				picture = "irakli_garibashvili.dds"
				ideology = socialism
				traits = {
					western_socialism
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { socialism_leader = 1 } }
			set_temp_variable = { b = 1 }
		}
	}
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 4 } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { communist_state_leader = 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { communist_state_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = GEO_public_soviet
				picture = "geo_soviet.dds"
				ideology = communist_state
				traits = {
					emerging_communist_state
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { communist_state_leader = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { date < 2016.1.2 } set_temp_variable = { b = 1 } } #skip if 2017
		}
	}
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 14 } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { Neutral_conservatism_leader = 0 } NOT = { check_variable = { b = 1 } } }
			add_to_variable = { Neutral_conservatism_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = GEO_zviad_dzidziguri
				picture = "zviad_dzidziguri.dds"
				ideology = Neutral_conservatism
				traits = {
					neutrality_Neutral_conservatism
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { Neutral_conservatism_leader = 1 } }
			set_temp_variable = { b = 1 }
		}
	}
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { conservatism_leader = 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { conservatism_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = GEO_irakli_alasania
				picture = "irakli_alasania.dds"
				ideology = conservatism
				traits = {
					western_conservatism
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { conservatism_leader = 1 } }
			set_temp_variable = { b = 1 }
		}
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { conservatism_leader = 1 } NOT = { check_variable = { b = 1 } } }
			add_to_variable = { conservatism_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = GEO_davit_bakradze
				picture = "davi_bakradze.dds"
				ideology = Western_Autocracy
				traits = {
					western_Western_Autocracy
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { conservatism_leader = 1 } }
			set_temp_variable = { b = 1 }
		}
	}
	else_if = { limit = { western_autocrats_are_in_power = yes }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { Western_Autocracy_leader = 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { Western_Autocracy_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = GEO_mikheil_saakashvili
				picture = "mikheil_saakashvili.dds"
				ideology = conservatism
				traits = {
					western_liberalism
					career_politician
					pro_american
					emotional
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { Western_Autocracy_leader = 1 } }
			set_temp_variable = { b = 1 }
		}
	}
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 6 } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { Conservative_leader = 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { Conservative_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = GEO_nino_burjanadze
				picture = "nino_burjanadze.dds"
				ideology = Conservative
				traits = {
					emerging_Conservative
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { Conservative_leader = 1 } }
			set_temp_variable = { b = 1 }
		}
	}
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 7 } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { Autocracy_leader = 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { Autocracy_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = GEO_davit_tarkhan_mouravi
				picture = "davit_tarkhan_mouravi.dds"
				ideology = Autocracy
				traits = {
					emerging_Autocracy
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { Autocracy_leader = 1 } }
			set_temp_variable = { b = 1 }
		}
	}
}

# --- GER_political_leaders.txt ---
set_leader_GER = {

	if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 1 } }

		if = { limit = { check_variable = { conservatism_leader = 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { conservatism_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = GER_angela_merkel
				picture = "Angela_Merkel.dds"
				ideology = conservatism
				traits = {
					scientist
					western_conservatism
					ruthless
					honest
					stubborn
					political_dancer
				}
			}

	if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 13 } } 
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { Neutral_Autocracy_leader = 0 } }
			set_variable = { Neutral_Autocracy_leader = 1 }
			kill_country_leader = yes

			create_country_leader = {
				name = ZZZ_nm_gna_mohamed_al_menfi
				picture = "gfx/leaders/CYR/Mohamed_al_Menfi.dds"
				ideology = Neutral_Autocracy
				traits = {
					neutrality_Neutral_Autocracy
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { Neutral_Autocracy_leader = 1 } }
			set_temp_variable = { b = 1 }
		}
	}
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 1 } } 
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { conservatism_leader = 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { conservatism_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = ZZZ_nm_gna_abdulrahman_sewehli
				desc = LBA_abdulrahman_sewehli_desc
				picture = "gfx/leaders/LBA/abdulrahman_sewehli.dds"
				ideology = conservatism
				traits = {
					western_conservatism
					defender_of_democracy
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { conservatism_leader = 1 } }
			set_temp_variable = { b = 1 }
		}
	}
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 2 } } 
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { liberalism_leader = 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { liberalism_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = ZZZ_nm_gna_ali_zeidan
				desc = LBA_ali_zeidan_desc

				ideology = liberalism
				traits = {
					western_liberalism
					lawyer

				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { liberalism_leader = 1 } }
			set_temp_variable = { b = 1 }
		}
	}

					neutrality_Neutral_conservatism
					political_flip_flopper

			if = { limit = { date < 2016.1.2 } set_temp_variable = { b = 1 } } #skip if 2017
		}
	}

	##########################

			add_to_variable = { neutral_Social_leader = 1 }

# --- JAM_political_leaders.txt ---
set_leader_JAM = {
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 2 } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { liberalism_leader = 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { liberalism_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = JAM_p_j_patterson
				picture = "pj_patterson.dds"
				ideology = liberalism
				traits = {
					western_liberalism
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { liberalism_leader = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { date < 2016.1.2 } set_temp_variable = { b = 1 } } #skip if 2017
		}
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { liberalism_leader = 1 } NOT = { check_variable = { b = 1 } } }
			add_to_variable = { liberalism_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = JAM_portia_simpson_miller
				picture = "Portia_Simpson-Miller.dds"
				ideology = liberalism
				traits = {
					western_liberalism
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { liberalism_leader = 1 } }
			set_temp_variable = { b = 1 }
		}
	}
}

					name = JAP_shigeru_ishiba
					picture = "shigeru_ishiba.dds"
					ideology = conservatism
					traits = {
						western_conservatism
					}
				}

				if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { conservatism_leader = 1 } }
			}

			else_if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { conservatism_leader = 2 } NOT = { check_variable = { b = 1 } }
				}
				add_to_variable = { conservatism_leader = 1 }
				hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }
				create_country_leader = {
					name = JAP_sanae_takaichi
					picture = "sanae_takaichi.dds"
					ideology = conservatism
					traits = {
						western_conservatism
					}
				}

				if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { conservatism_leader = 1 } }
			}
		}

		else_if = {
			limit = {
				date < 2025.10.4
				date > 2024.9.30
			}

			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { conservatism_leader = 0 } NOT = { check_variable = { b = 1 } }
				}
				add_to_variable = { conservatism_leader = 1 }
				hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }
				create_country_leader = {
					name = JAP_shigeru_ishiba
					picture = "shigeru_ishiba.dds"
					ideology = conservatism
					traits = {
						western_conservatism
					}
				}

				if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { conservatism_leader = 1 } }
			}

			else_if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { conservatism_leader = 1 } NOT = { check_variable = { b = 1 } }
				}
				add_to_variable = { conservatism_leader = 1 }
				hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }
				create_country_leader = {
					name = JAP_sanae_takaichi
					picture = "sanae_takaichi.dds"
					ideology = conservatism
					traits = {
						western_conservatism
					}
				}

				if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { conservatism_leader = 1 } }
			}
		}

		else_if = {
			limit = {
				date > 2025.10.3
			}

			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { conservatism_leader = 0 } NOT = { check_variable = { b = 1 } }
				}
				add_to_variable = { conservatism_leader = 1 }
				hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }
				create_country_leader = {
						name = JAP_sanae_takaichi
						picture = "sanae_takaichi.dds"
						ideology = conservatism
						traits = {
							western_conservatism
						}
					}

				if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { conservatism_leader = 1 } }
			}
		}
	}
}

# --- KOR_political_leaders.txt ---
set_leader_KOR = {

	if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 1 } }

		# 按年份直建：任一时间点只命中一档，避免原链在 2017 一次性连建到洪准杓
		if = { limit = { date < 2004.1.2 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = KOR_lee_hoi_chang
				picture = "Lee_Hoi-chang.dds"
				ideology = conservatism
				traits = {
					western_conservatism
					pro_american
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = { limit = { date < 2013.2.25 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = KOR_lee_myung_bak
				desc = LEE_MYUNG_BAK_LEADER_DESC
				picture = "lee_myung_bak.dds"
				ideology = conservatism
				traits = {
					western_conservatism
					geopolitical_thinker
					pro_american
					businessman
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = { limit = { date < 2016.12.9 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = KOR_park_geun_hye
				desc = PARK_GEUN_HYE_LEADER_DESC
				picture = "Park_Geun-hye.dds"
				ideology = conservatism
				traits = {
					western_conservatism
					deceitful
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = { limit = { date < 2022.5.10 }
			# 黄教安：2016.12.9 代总统起，保守派在野期继续作为该派领袖，直到 2022.5.10 尹锡悦就任
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = KOR_hwang_kyo_ahn

				ideology = conservatism
				traits = {
					western_conservatism

			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

		if = { limit = { check_variable = { conservatism_leader = 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { conservatism_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = LIT_valdas_adamkus
				picture = "valdas_adamkus.dds"
				ideology = conservatism
				traits = {
					western_conservatism
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { conservatism_leader = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { date < 2016.1.2 } set_temp_variable = { b = 1 } } #skip if 2017
		}
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { conservatism_leader = 1 } NOT = { check_variable = { b = 1 } } }
			add_to_variable = { conservatism_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = LIT_gabrielius_landsbergis
				picture = "gabrielius_landsbergis.dds"
				ideology = conservatism
				traits = {
					western_conservatism
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { conservatism_leader = 1 } }
			set_temp_variable = { b = 1 }
		}
	}
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 16 } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { Neutral_Libertarian_leader = 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { Neutral_Libertarian_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = LIT_loreta_grauziniene
				picture = "loreta_grauziniene.dds"
				ideology = Neutral_Libertarian
				traits = {
					neutrality_Neutral_Libertarian
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { Neutral_Libertarian_leader = 1 } }
			set_temp_variable = { b = 1 }
		}
	}
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 6 } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { Conservative_leader = 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { Conservative_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = LIT_sergey_dmitriev
				picture = "sergey_dmitriev.dds"
				ideology = Conservative
				traits = {
					emerging_Conservative
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { Conservative_leader = 1 } }
			set_temp_variable = { b = 1 }
		}
	}
}

set_leader_LKT = {

	###########################
	#### Western Conservative ####
	###########################
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { conservatism_leader = 0 } NOT = { check_variable = { b = 1 } } }
			add_to_variable = { conservatism_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = LKT_bill_graves
				picture = "bill_graves.dds"
				ideology = conservatism
				traits = {
					western_conservatism
					businessman
					union_man
					compassionate_gentleman
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { conservatism_leader = 1 } }
		}
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { conservatism_leader = 1 } NOT = { check_variable = { b = 1 } } }
			add_to_variable = { conservatism_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = LKT_mike_johanns
				picture = "mike_johns.dds"
				ideology = conservatism
				traits = {
					western_conservatism
					lawyer
					career_politician
					likeable
				}
			}

		}
	}

	##########################
	##### Western Socialism #####
	##########################
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 3 } }

			add_to_variable = { socalism_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

				traits = {
					western_socialism
					agronomist

					compassionate_gentleman
				}
			}

		}
	}

	############################
	#### Emerging Autocrats ####
	############################
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 7 } }

			add_to_variable = { Autocracy_leader = 1 }

				name = LKT_mike_rounds
				picture = "mike_rounds.dds"

				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { Autocracy_leader = 1 } }
		}
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { Autocracy_leader = 1 } NOT = { check_variable = { b = 1 } } }
			add_to_variable = { Autocracy_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = LKT_bill_janklow
				picture = "bill_janklow.dds"

		}
	}

	################################

	################################

                    emerging_Autocracy
                }
            }
        }
    }

set_leader_LUX = {

	if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 1 } }

		if = { limit = { check_variable = { conservatism_leader = 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { conservatism_leader = 1 }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = LUX_robert_mehlen
				picture = "robert_mehlen.dds"
				ideology = conservatism
				traits = {
					western_conservatism
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { conservatism_leader = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { date < 2016.1.2 } set_temp_variable = { b = 1 } } #skip if 2017
		}
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { conservatism_leader = 1 } NOT = { check_variable = { b = 1 } } }
			add_to_variable = { conservatism_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }
